We are aware that people do use parts as such,\nand we do not put any deliberate inconvenience in the way, but we want you to be\naware that no care has been taken to make it work well as a library. <a href="https://bugs.chromium.org/p/oss-fuzz/issues/list?sort=-opened&amp;can=1&amp;q=proj:prometheus" rel="nofollow"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/00899a49acb06e5e62fe97f61913b2e29f2dd41411f5f437ecb6e983ae7268f2/68747470733a2f2f6f73732d66757a7a2d6275696c642d6c6f67732e73746f726167652e676f6f676c65617069732e636f6d2f6261646765732f70726f6d6574686575732e737667" alt="Fuzzing Status" data-canonical-src="https://oss-fuzz-build-logs.storage.googleapis.com/badges/prometheus.svg" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
</div>
<p dir="auto">Prometheus, a <a href="https://cncf.io/" rel="nofollow">Cloud Native Computing Foundation</a> project, is a systems and service monitoring system. It collects metrics
from configured targets at given intervals, evaluates rule expressions,
displays the results, and can trigger alerts when specified conditions are observed.</p>
<p dir="auto">The features that distinguish Prometheus from other metrics and monitoring systems are:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>A <strong>multi-dimensional</strong> data model (time series defined by metric name and set of key/value dimensions)</li>
<li>PromQL, a <strong>powerful and flexible query language</strong> to leverage this dimensionality</li>
<li>No dependency on distributed storage; <strong>single server nodes are autonomous</strong></li>
<li>An HTTP <strong>pull model</strong> for time series collection</li>
<li><strong>Pushing time series</strong> is supported via an intermediary gateway for batch jobs</li>
<li>Targets are discovered via <strong>service discovery</strong> or <strong>static configuration</strong></li>
<li>Multiple modes of <strong>graphing and dashboarding support</strong></li>
<li>Support for hierarchical and horizontal <strong>federation</strong></li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Architecture overview</h2><a id="user-content-architecture-overview" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Architecture overview" href="#architecture-overview"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="/prometheus/prometheus/blob/main/documentation/images/architecture.svg"><img src="/prometheus/prometheus/raw/main/documentation/images/architecture.svg" alt="Architecture overview" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Install</h2><a id="user-content-install" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Install" href="#install"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">There are various ways to install Prometheus.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Precompiled binaries</h3><a id="user-content-precompiled-binaries" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Precompiled binaries" href="#precompiled-binaries"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Precompiled binaries for released versions are available in the
<a href="https://prometheus.io/download/" rel="nofollow"><em>download</em> section</a>
on <a href="https://prometheus.io" rel="nofollow">prometheus.io</a>. Using the latest production release binary
is the recommended way to install Prometheus.
See the <a href="https://prometheus.io/docs/introduction/install/" rel="nofollow">Installing</a>
chapter in the documentation for all the details.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Docker images</h3><a id="user-content-docker-images" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Docker images" href="#docker-images"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Docker images are available on <a href="https://quay.io/repository/prometheus/prometheus" rel="nofollow">Quay.io</a> or <a href="https://hub.docker.com/r/prom/prometheus/" rel="nofollow">Docker Hub</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">You can launch a Prometheus container for trying it out with</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker run --name prometheus -d -p 127.0.0.1:9090:9090 prom/prometheus"><pre>docker run --name prometheus -d -p 127.0.0.1:9090:9090 prom/prometheus</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Prometheus will now be reachable at <a href="http://localhost:9090/" rel="nofollow">http://localhost:9090/</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Building from source</h3><a id="user-content-building-from-source" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Building from source" href="#building-from-source"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">To build Prometheus from source code, you need:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Go: Version specified in <a href="/prometheus/prometheus/blob/main/go.mod">go.mod</a> or greater.</li>
<li>NodeJS: Version specified in <a href="/prometheus/prometheus/blob/main/web/ui/.nvmrc">.nvmrc</a> or greater.</li>
<li>npm: Version 10 or greater (check with <code>npm --version</code> and <a href="https://www.npmjs.com/" rel="nofollow">here</a>).</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Start by cloning the repository:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="git clone https://github.com/prometheus/prometheus.git
cd prometheus"><pre>git clone https://github.com/prometheus/prometheus.git
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> prometheus</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">You can use the <code>go</code> tool to build and install the <code>prometheus</code>
and <code>promtool</code> binaries into your <code>GOPATH</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="go install github.com/prometheus/prometheus/cmd/...
prometheus --config.file=your_config.yml"><pre>go install github.com/prometheus/prometheus/cmd/...
prometheus --config.file=your_config.yml</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><em>However</em>, when using <code>go install</code> to build Prometheus, Prometheus will expect to be able to
read its web assets from local filesystem directories under <code>web/ui/static</code>. In order for
these assets to be found, you will have to run Prometheus from the root of the cloned
repository. Note also that this directory does not include the React UI unless it has been
built explicitly using <code>make assets</code> or <code>make build</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">An example of the above configuration file can be found <a href="https://github.com/prometheus/prometheus/blob/main/documentation/examples/prometheus.yml">here.</a></p>
<p dir="auto">You can also build using <code>make build</code>, which will compile in the web assets so that
Prometheus can be run from anywhere:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make build
./prometheus --config.file=your_config.yml"><pre>make build
./prometheus --config.file=your_config.yml</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The Makefile provides several targets:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><em>build</em>: build the <code>prometheus</code> and <code>promtool</code> binaries (includes building and compiling in web assets)</li>
<li><em>test</em>: run the tests</li>
<li><em>test-short</em>: run the short tests</li>
<li><em>format</em>: format the source code</li>
<li><em>vet</em>: check the source code for common errors</li>
<li><em>assets</em>: build the React UI</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Service discovery plugins</h3><a id="user-content-service-discovery-plugins" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Service discovery plugins" href="#service-discovery-plugins"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Prometheus is bundled with many service discovery plugins. You can customize
which service discoveries are included in your build using Go build tags.</p>
<p dir="auto">To exclude service discoveries when building with <code>make build</code>, add the desired
tags to the <code>.promu.yml</code> file under <code>build.tags.all</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-yaml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="build:
    tags:
        all:
            - netgo
            - builtinassets
            - remove_all_sd           # Exclude all optional SDs
            - enable_kubernetes_sd    # Re-enable only kubernetes"><pre><span class="pl-ent">build</span>:
    <span class="pl-ent">tags</span>:
        <span class="pl-ent">all</span>:
            - <span class="pl-s">netgo</span>
            - <span class="pl-s">builtinassets</span>
            - <span class="pl-s">remove_all_sd           </span><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Exclude all optional SDs</span>
            - <span class="pl-s">enable_kubernetes_sd    </span><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Re-enable only kubernetes</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Then run <code>make build</code> as usual. Alternatively, when using <code>go build</code> directly:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="go build -tags &quot;remove_all_sd,enable_kubernetes_sd&quot; ./cmd/prometheus"><pre>go build -tags <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>remove_all_sd,enable_kubernetes_sd<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> ./cmd/prometheus</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Available build tags:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>remove_all_sd</code> - Exclude all optional service discoveries (keeps file_sd, static_sd, and http_sd)</li>
<li><code>enable_&lt;name&gt;_sd</code> - Re-enable a specific SD when using <code>remove_all_sd</code></li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">If you add out-of-tree plugins, which we do not endorse at the moment,
additional steps might be needed to adjust the <code>go.mod</code> and <code>go.sum</code> files. As
always, be extra careful when loading third party code.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Building the Docker image</h3><a id="user-content-building-the-docker-image" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Building the Docker image" href="#building-the-docker-image"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">You can build a docker image locally with the following commands:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make promu
promu crossbuild -p linux/amd64
make common-docker-amd64"><pre>make promu
promu crossbuild -p linux/amd64
make common-docker-amd64</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The <code>make docker</code> target is intended only for use in our CI system and will not
produce a fully working image when run locally.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Using Prometheus as a Go Library</h2><a id="user-content-using-prometheus-as-a-go-library" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Using Prometheus as a Go Library" href="#using-prometheus-as-a-go-library"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Within the Prometheus project, repositories such as <a href="https://github.com/prometheus/common">prometheus/common</a> and
<a href="https://github.com/prometheus/client-golang">prometheus/client-golang</a> are designed as re-usable libraries.</p>
<p dir="auto">The <a href="https://github.com/prometheus/prometheus">prometheus/prometheus</a> repository builds a stand-alone program and is not
designed for use as a library. We are aware that people do use parts as such,
and we do not put any deliberate inconvenience in the way, but we want you to be
aware that no care has been taken to make it work well as a library. For instance,
you may encounter errors that only surface when used as a library.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Remote Write</h3><a id="user-content-remote-write" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Remote Write" href="#remote-write"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">We are publishing our Remote Write protobuf independently at
<a href="https://buf.build/prometheus/prometheus/assets" rel="nofollow">buf.build</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">You can use that as a library:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="go get buf.build/gen/go/prometheus/prometheus/protocolbuffers/go@latest"><pre>go get buf.build/gen/go/prometheus/prometheus/protocolbuffers/go@latest</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">This is experimental.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Prometheus code base</h3><a id="user-content-prometheus-code-base" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Prometheus code base" href="#prometheus-code-base"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">In order to comply with <a href="https://go.dev/ref/mod#versions" rel="nofollow">go mod</a> rules,
Prometheus release number do not exactly match Go module releases.</p>
<p dir="auto">For the
Prometheus v3.y.z releases, we are publishing equivalent v0.3y.z tags. The y in v0.3y.z is always padded to two digits, with a leading zero if needed.</p>
<p dir="auto">Therefore, a user that would want to use Prometheus v3.0.0 as a library could do:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="go get github.com/prometheus/prometheus@v0.300.0"><pre>go get github.com/prometheus/prometheus@v0.300.0</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">For the
Prometheus v2.y.z releases, we published the equivalent v0.y.z tags.</p>
<p dir="auto">Therefore, a user that would want to use Prometheus v2.35.0 as a library could do:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="go get github.com/prometheus/prometheus@v0.35.0"><pre>go get github.com/prometheus/prometheus@v0.35.0</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">This solution makes it clear that we might break our internal Go APIs between
minor user-facing releases, as <a href="https://semver.org/#spec-item-4" rel="nofollow">breaking changes are allowed in major version
zero</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">React UI Development</h2><a id="user-content-react-ui-development" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: React UI Development" href="#react-ui-development"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">For more information on building, running, and developing on the React-based UI, see the React app's <a href="/prometheus/prometheus/blob/main/web/ui/README.md">README.md</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">More information</h2><a id="user-content-more-information" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: More information" href="#more-information"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Godoc documentation is available via <a href="https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/prometheus/prometheus" rel="nofollow">pkg.go.dev</a>. Due to peculiarities of Go Modules, v3.y.z will be displayed as v0.3y.z (the y in v0.3y.z is always padded to two digits, with a leading zero if needed), while v2.y.z will be displayed as v0.y.z.</li>
<li>See the <a href="https://prometheus.io/community" rel="nofollow">Community page</a> for how to reach the Prometheus developers and users on various communication channels.</li>
</ul>
